{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0171rtSZcQ3tQDcr15R3HiYT","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# StellarStage Carnival – Clean Architecture Blueprint\n     2→\n     3→_This document is a living, executable specification of how StellarStage Carnival’s JavaScript / TypeScript code-base is organised.  All code blocks are copy-paste ready for production and every design decision is mapped to a concrete implementation detail._\n     4→\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→## 1. Layered Onion\n     8→\n     9→```mermaid\n    10→graph LR\n    11→    A[Presentation<br/>React + Three.js] --> B[Application<br/>GraphQL / WebSocket Gateway]\n    12→    B --> C[Use-Cases<br/>Orchestrators]\n    13→    C --> D[Domain<br/>Entities & Value Objects]\n    14→    C --> E[Domain Services<br/>Factories, Strategies, State-Machines]\n    15→    C --> F[Event Bus<br/>Observer Pattern]\n    16→    C --> G[Port Adapters<br/>Ethereum, IPFS, Streaming]\n    17→    G --> H[External Systems<br/>L1/L2, CDN, Storage]\n    18→```\n    19→\n    20→* Nothing in `Domain` depends on outer layers.  \n    21→* `Use-Cases` know only the `Domain` and well-defined `Ports`.  \n    22→* `Adapters` implement the `Ports`, allowing for painless chain upgrades.\n    23→\n    24→---\n    25→\n    26→## 2. Domain Layer (Pure, No Side-Effects)\n    27→\n    28→### 2.1 Value Objects\n    29→\n    30→```ts\n    31→// src/domain/value-objects/Address.ts\n    32→export class Address {\n    33→  constructor(private readonly value: string) {\n    34→    if (!/^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$/.test(value)) {\n    35→      throw new Error(`Invalid Ethereum address ${value}`);\n    36→    }\n    37→  }\n    38→  equals(other: Address): boolean {\n    39→    return this.value.toLowerCase() === other.value.toLowerCase();\n    40→  }\n    41→  toString(): string {\n    42→    return this.value;\n    43→  }\n    44→}\n    45→```\n    46→\n    47→### 2.2 Entities\n    48→\n    49→```ts\n    50→// src/domain/entities/Show.ts\n    51→import { AggregateRoot } from '../shared/AggregateRoot';\n    52→import { Address } from '../value-objects/Address';\n    53→\n    54→export interface ActProps {\n    55→  id: string;\n    56→  name: string;\n    57→  performerAddr: Address;\n    58→  startAt: Date;\n    59→  endAt: Date;\n    60→}\n    61→\n    62→export interface ShowProps {\n    63→  id: string;\n    64→  title: string;\n    65→  owner: Address;\n    66→  acts: ActProps[];\n    67→}\n    68→\n    69→export class Show extends AggregateRoot<ShowProps> {\n    70→  get id() { return this.props.id; }\n    71→  get acts() { return this.props.acts; }\n    72→\n    73→  addAct(act: ActProps) {\n    74→    if (this.props.acts.find(a => a.id === act.id)) {\n    75→      throw new Error(`Act ${act.id} already exists`);\n    76→    }\n    77→    this.props.acts.push(act);\n    78→    this.addDomainEvent({ type: 'ActAdded', payload: act });\n    79→  }\n    80→}\n    81→```\n    82→\n    83→---\n    84→\n    85→## 3. Application Layer (Use-Cases)\n    86→\n    87→### 3.1 Port Definitions\n    88→\n    89→```ts\n    90→// src/application/ports/blockchain/NftGateway.ts\n    91→import { Pass } from '../../domain/entities/Pass';\n    92→\n    93→export interface NftGateway {\n    94→  mintPass(pass: Pass, to: string): Promise<string>; // returns tx hash\n    95→  upgradePass(tokenId: string, traits: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;\n    96→}\n    97→```\n    98→\n    99→### 3.2 Use-Case Service Example – MintShowPass\n   100→\n   101→```ts\n   102→// src/application/use-cases/MintShowPass.ts\n   103→import { ShowRepository } from '../ports/repositories/ShowRepository';\n   104→import { PassFactory } from '../services/PassFactory';\n   105→import { NftGateway } from '../ports/blockchain/NftGateway';\n   106→import { EventBus } from '../ports/event/EventBus';\n   107→\n   108→export class MintShowPass {\n   109→  constructor(\n   110→    private readonly showRepo: ShowRepository,\n   111→    private readonly passFactory: PassFactory,\n   112→    private readonly nftGateway: NftGateway,\n   113→    private readonly bus: EventBus,\n   114→  ) {}\n   115→\n   116→  async execute(cmd: { showId: string; userWallet: string }) {\n   117→    const show = await this.showRepo.byId(cmd.showId);\n   118→    if (!show) throw new Error('Show not found');\n   119→\n   120→    const pass = this.passFactory.createForShow(show, cmd.userWallet);\n   121→\n   122→    const txHash = await this.nftGateway.mintPass(pass, cmd.userWallet);\n   123→    await this.showRepo.savePass(pass);\n   124→\n   125→    this.bus.publish({ type: 'PassMinted', payload: { txHash, passId: pass.id } });\n   126→    return { txHash, passId: pass.id };\n   127→  }\n   128→}\n   129→```\n   130→\n   131→---\n   132→\n   133→## 4. Domain Services\n   134→\n   135→### 4.1 Factory Pattern – PassFactory\n   136→\n   137→```ts\n   138→// src/application/services/PassFactory.ts\n   139→import { ulid } from 'ulid';\n   140→import { Pass } from '../../domain/entities/Pass';\n   141→import { Show } from '../../domain/entities/Show';\n   142→\n   143→export class PassFactory {\n   144→  createForShow(show: Show, ownerWallet: string): Pass {\n   145→    return new Pass({\n   146→      id: ulid(),\n   147→      showId: show.id,\n   148→      ownerWallet,\n   149→      level: 1,\n   150→      traits: this.defaultTraits(show),\n   151→    });\n   152→  }\n   153→\n   154→  private defaultTraits(show: Show) {\n   155→    return {\n   156→      theme: show.title.toLowerCase().includes('comedy') ? 'funny' : 'epic',\n   157→      rarityScore: Math.random() * 100,\n   158→    };\n   159→  }\n   160→}\n   161→```\n   162→\n   163→---\n   164→\n   165→## 5. Infrastructure Layer\n   166→\n   167→### 5.1 Ethereum Adapter (uses ethers.js)\n   168→\n   169→```ts\n   170→// src/infrastructure/blockchain/EthersNftGateway.ts\n   171→import { ethers } from 'ethers';\n   172→import { NftGateway } from '../../application/ports/blockchain/NftGateway';\n   173→import abi from './StellarPass.json';\n   174→\n   175→export class EthersNftGateway implements NftGateway {\n   176→  private contract: ethers.Contract;\n   177→\n   178→  constructor(\n   179→    private readonly provider: ethers.providers.JsonRpcProvider,\n   180→    private readonly signer: ethers.Signer,\n   181→    contractAddress: string,\n   182→  ) {\n   183→    this.contract = new ethers.Contract(contractAddress, abi, signer);\n   184→  }\n   185→\n   186→  async mintPass(pass, to) {\n   187→    const tx = await this.contract.safeMint(to, pass.metadataUri);\n   188→    await tx.wait(2);\n   189→    return tx.hash;\n   190→  }\n   191→\n   192→  async upgradePass(tokenId, traits) {\n   193→    const tx = await this.contract.setTraits(tokenId, JSON.stringify(traits));\n   194→    await tx.wait(1);\n   195→  }\n   196→}\n   197→```\n   198→\n   199→### 5.2 Event Bus Adapter\n   200→\n   201→```ts\n   202→// src/infrastructure/event/WebSocketEventBus.ts\n   203→import WebSocket from 'ws';\n   204→import { EventBus, DomainEvent } from '../../application/ports/event/EventBus';\n   205→\n   206→export class WebSocketEventBus implements EventBus {\n   207→  private readonly wss = new WebSocket.Server({ noServer: true });\n   208→  private readonly listeners: Set<(e: DomainEvent) => void> = new Set();\n   209→\n   210→  publish(event: DomainEvent): void {\n   211→    const json = JSON.stringify(event);\n   212→    this.listeners.forEach(cb => cb(event));\n   213→    this.wss.clients.forEach(client => client.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN && client.send(json));\n   214→  }\n   215→\n   216→  subscribe(cb: (e: DomainEvent) => void): void {\n   217→    this.listeners.add(cb);\n   218→  }\n   219→}\n   220→```\n   221→\n   222→---\n   223→\n   224→## 6. Reactive Pipeline (Observer → Strategy → State-Machine)\n   225→\n   226→```ts\n   227→// src/application/services/ShowStateMachine.ts\n   228→import { createMachine, interpret } from 'xstate';\n   229→import { EventBus, DomainEvent } from '../ports/event/EventBus';\n   230→\n   231→export class ShowStateMachine {\n   232→  private readonly service;\n   233→\n   234→  constructor(private bus: EventBus) {\n   235→    const machine = createMachine({\n   236→      id: 'show',\n   237→      initial: 'scheduled',\n   238→      states: {\n   239→        scheduled: { on: { ActStarted: 'live' } },\n   240→        live: { on: { ActEnded: 'break' } },\n   241→        break: { on: { ActStarted: 'live', ShowEnded: 'ended' } },\n   242→        ended: { type: 'final' },\n   243→      },\n   244→    });\n   245→\n   246→    this.service = interpret(machine).onTransition(state => {\n   247→      bus.publish({ type: 'ShowStateChanged', payload: { state: state.value } });\n   248→    });\n   249→    this.bus.subscribe(this.onEvent.bind(this));\n   250→  }\n   251→\n   252→  start() { this.service.start(); }\n   253→\n   254→  private onEvent(evt: DomainEvent) {\n   255→    this.service.send(evt.type as any);\n   256→  }\n   257→}\n   258→```\n   259→\n   260→---\n   261→\n   262→## 7. Proxy Smart Contracts (Upgrade-safe)\n   263→\n   264→```solidity\n   265→// contracts/StellarPassProxy.sol (snippet)\n   266→contract StellarPassProxy is ERC1967Proxy {\n   267→    constructor(address _logic, bytes memory _data)\n   268→        ERC1967Proxy(_logic, _data) {}\n   269→}\n   270→```\n   271→\n   272→_Key points_\n   273→\n   274→1. Implementation logic lives in `StellarPass.sol`.\n   275→2. Proxy delegates calls, preserving storage layout for future upgrades.\n   276→3. Royalties enforced via EIP-2981 in implementation contract.\n   277→\n   278→---\n   279→\n   280→## 8. Persistence & CQRS\n   281→\n   282→* Command side uses a fast Hot-Path DB (PostgreSQL + Prisma).\n   283→* Query side is materialised into Apollo GraphQL subgraphs.  \n   284→* Both are kept in sync via the Event Bus to ensure eventual consistency.\n   285→\n   286→---\n   287→\n   288→## 9. Observability & Error Handling\n   289→\n   290→```ts\n   291→// src/infrastructure/logging/pinoLogger.ts\n   292→import pino from 'pino';\n   293→export const logger = pino({ level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL ?? 'info' });\n   294→\n   295→// Example usage\n   296→try {\n   297→  await nftGateway.mintPass(pass, wallet);\n   298→} catch (err) {\n   299→  logger.error({ err, passId: pass.id }, 'Failed to mint pass');\n   300→  throw new MintPassError(pass.id, err);\n   301→}\n   302→```\n   303→\n   304→---\n   305→\n   306→## 10. Testing Strategy\n   307→\n   308→1. Domain & Use-Cases – pure unit tests (Jest).  \n   309→2. Contract tests for Ports (pact-style mocks).  \n   310→3. Solidity contracts – Foundry + Hardhat coverage > 95 %.  \n   311→4. End-to-End – Playwright + Ganache snapshotting.\n   312→\n   313→```ts\n   314→// tests/use-cases/mintShowPass.spec.ts\n   315→describe('MintShowPass', () => {\n   316→  it('mints and publishes event', async () => {\n   317→    const result = await useCase.execute({ showId, userWallet });\n   318→    expect(result.passId).toBeDefined();\n   319→    expect(eventSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ type: 'PassMinted' }));\n   320→  });\n   321→});\n   322→```\n   323→\n   324→---\n   325→\n   326→## 11. Security Blueprint\n   327→\n   328→* All off-chain messages signed via EIP-712.  \n   329→* On-chain re-entrancy guarded.  \n   330→* Role-based access (`DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE`, `MINTER_ROLE`).  \n   331→* Continuous audit pipeline with Slither + MythX.\n   332→\n   333→---\n   334→\n   335→## 12. Deployment Matrix\n   336→\n   337→| Env | Chain | Storage | Endpoint                  |\n   338→|-----|-------|---------|---------------------------|\n   339→| dev | Hardhat | local IPFS | http://localhost:4000 |\n   340→| staging | Polygon Mumbai | Pinata | https://staging.api.stellar | \n   341→| prod | Ethereum Mainnet | Filecoin | https://api.stellar |\n   342→\n   343→---\n   344→\n   345→> The architecture is designed to remain chain-agnostic: swapping Ethereum for Solana is merely a matter of implementing the same Ports in a new Adapter without touching business rules.\n   346→\n   347→_This concludes the blueprint._\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. All code blocks are copy-paste ready for production and every design decision is mapped to a concrete implementation detail._\n\n---\n\n## 1. Layered Onion\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    A[Presentation<br/>React + Three.js] --> B[Application<br/>GraphQL / WebSocket Gateway]\n    B --> C[Use-Cases<br/>Orchestrators]\n    C --> D[Domain<br/>Entities & Value Objects]\n    C --> E[Domain Services<br/>Factories, Strategies, State-Machines]\n    C --> F[Event Bus<br/>Observer Pattern]\n    C --> G[Port Adapters<br/>Ethereum, IPFS, Streaming]\n    G --> H[External Systems<br/>L1/L2, CDN, Storage]\n```\n\n* Nothing in `Domain` depends on outer layers.  \n* `Use-Cases` know only the `Domain` and well-defined `Ports`.  \n* `Adapters` implement the `Ports`, allowing for painless chain upgrades.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Domain Layer (Pure, No Side-Effects)\n\n### 2.1 Value Objects\n\n```ts\n// src/domain/value-objects/Address.ts\nexport class Address {\n  constructor(private readonly value: string) {\n    if (!/^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$/.test(value)) {\n      throw new Error(`Invalid Ethereum address ${value}`);\n    }\n  }\n  equals(other: Address): boolean {\n    return this.value.toLowerCase() === other.value.toLowerCase();\n  }\n  toString(): string {\n    return this.value;\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 2.2 Entities\n\n```ts\n// src/domain/entities/Show.ts\nimport { AggregateRoot } from '../shared/AggregateRoot';\nimport { Address } from '../value-objects/Address';\n\nexport interface ActProps {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  performerAddr: Address;\n  startAt: Date;\n  endAt: Date;\n}\n\nexport interface ShowProps {\n  id: string;\n  title: string;\n  owner: Address;\n  acts: ActProps[];\n}\n\nexport class Show extends AggregateRoot<ShowProps> {\n  get id() { return this.props.id; }\n  get acts() { return this.props.acts; }\n\n  addAct(act: ActProps) {\n    if (this.props.acts.find(a => a.id === act.id)) {\n      throw new Error(`Act ${act.id} already exists`);\n    }\n    this.props.acts.push(act);\n    this.addDomainEvent({ type: 'ActAdded', payload: act });\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Application Layer (Use-Cases)\n\n### 3.1 Port Definitions\n\n```ts\n// src/application/ports/blockchain/NftGateway.ts\nimport { Pass } from '../../domain/entities/Pass';\n\nexport interface NftGateway {\n  mintPass(pass: Pass, to: string): Promise<string>; // returns tx hash\n  upgradePass(tokenId: string, traits: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;\n}\n```\n\n### 3.2 Use-Case Service Example – MintShowPass\n\n```ts\n// src/application/use-cases/MintShowPass.ts\nimport { ShowRepository } from '../ports/repositories/ShowRepository';\nimport { PassFactory } from '../services/PassFactory';\nimport { NftGateway } from '../ports/blockchain/NftGateway';\nimport { EventBus } from '../ports/event/EventBus';\n\nexport class MintShowPass {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly showRepo: ShowRepository,\n    private readonly passFactory: PassFactory,\n    private readonly nftGateway: NftGateway,\n    private readonly bus: EventBus,\n  ) {}\n\n  async execute(cmd: { showId: string; userWallet: string }) {\n    const show = await this.showRepo.byId(cmd.showId);\n    if (!show) throw new Error('Show not found');\n\n    const pass = this.passFactory.createForShow(show, cmd.userWallet);\n\n    const txHash = await this.nftGateway.mintPass(pass, cmd.userWallet);\n    await this.showRepo.savePass(pass);\n\n    this.bus.publish({ type: 'PassMinted', payload: { txHash, passId: pass.id } });\n    return { txHash, passId: pass.id };\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Domain Services\n\n### 4.1 Factory Pattern – PassFactory\n\n```ts\n// src/application/services/PassFactory.ts\nimport { ulid } from 'ulid';\nimport { Pass } from '../../domain/entities/Pass';\nimport { Show } from '../../domain/entities/Show';\n\nexport class PassFactory {\n  createForShow(show: Show, ownerWallet: string): Pass {\n    return new Pass({\n      id: ulid(),\n      showId: show.id,\n      ownerWallet,\n      level: 1,\n      traits: this.defaultTraits(show),\n    });\n  }\n\n  private defaultTraits(show: Show) {\n    return {\n      theme: show.title.toLowerCase().includes('comedy') ? 'funny' : 'epic',\n      rarityScore: Math.random() * 100,\n    };\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Infrastructure Layer\n\n### 5.1 Ethereum Adapter (uses ethers.js)\n\n```ts\n// src/infrastructure/blockchain/EthersNftGateway.ts\nimport { ethers } from 'ethers';\nimport { NftGateway } from '../../application/ports/blockchain/NftGateway';\nimport abi from './StellarPass.json';\n\nexport class EthersNftGateway implements NftGateway {\n  private contract: ethers.Contract;\n\n  constructor(\n    private readonly provider: ethers.providers.JsonRpcProvider,\n    private readonly signer: ethers.Signer,\n    contractAddress: string,\n  ) {\n    this.contract = new ethers.Contract(contractAddress, abi, signer);\n  }\n\n  async mintPass(pass, to) {\n    const tx = await this.contract.safeMint(to, pass.metadataUri);\n    await tx.wait(2);\n    return tx.hash;\n  }\n\n  async upgradePass(tokenId, traits) {\n    const tx = await this.contract.setTraits(tokenId, JSON.stringify(traits));\n    await tx.wait(1);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 5.2 Event Bus Adapter\n\n```ts\n// src/infrastructure/event/WebSocketEventBus.ts\nimport WebSocket from 'ws';\nimport { EventBus, DomainEvent } from '../../application/ports/event/EventBus';\n\nexport class WebSocketEventBus implements EventBus {\n  private readonly wss = new WebSocket.Server({ noServer: true });\n  private readonly listeners: Set<(e: DomainEvent) => void> = new Set();\n\n  publish(event: DomainEvent): void {\n    const json = JSON.stringify(event);\n    this.listeners.forEach(cb => cb(event));\n    this.wss.clients.forEach(client => client.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN && client.send(json));\n  }\n\n  subscribe(cb: (e: DomainEvent) => void): void {\n    this.listeners.add(cb);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Reactive Pipeline (Observer → Strategy → State-Machine)\n\n```ts\n// src/application/services/ShowStateMachine.ts\nimport { createMachine, interpret } from 'xstate';\nimport { EventBus, DomainEvent } from '../ports/event/EventBus';\n\nexport class ShowStateMachine {\n  private readonly service;\n\n  constructor(private bus: EventBus) {\n    const machine = createMachine({\n      id: 'show',\n      initial: 'scheduled',\n      states: {\n        scheduled: { on: { ActStarted: 'live' } },\n        live: { on: { ActEnded: 'break' } },\n        break: { on: { ActStarted: 'live', ShowEnded: 'ended' } },\n        ended: { type: 'final' },\n      },\n    });\n\n    this.service = interpret(machine).onTransition(state => {\n      bus.publish({ type: 'ShowStateChanged', payload: { state: state.value } });\n    });\n    this.bus.subscribe(this.onEvent.bind(this));\n  }\n\n  start() { this.service.start(); }\n\n  private onEvent(evt: DomainEvent) {\n    this.service.send(evt.type as any);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Proxy Smart Contracts (Upgrade-safe)\n\n```solidity\n// contracts/StellarPassProxy.sol (snippet)\ncontract StellarPassProxy is ERC1967Proxy {\n    constructor(address _logic, bytes memory _data)\n        ERC1967Proxy(_logic, _data) {}\n}\n```\n\n_Key points_\n\n1. Implementation logic lives in `StellarPass.sol`.\n2. Proxy delegates calls, preserving storage layout for future upgrades.\n3. Royalties enforced via EIP-2981 in implementation contract.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Persistence & CQRS\n\n* Command side uses a fast Hot-Path DB (PostgreSQL + Prisma).\n* Query side is materialised into Apollo GraphQL subgraphs.  \n* Both are kept in sync via the Event Bus to ensure eventual consistency.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Observability & Error Handling\n\n```ts\n// src/infrastructure/logging/pinoLogger.ts\nimport pino from 'pino';\nexport const logger = pino({ level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL ?? 'info' });\n\n// Example usage\ntry {\n  await nftGateway.mintPass(pass, wallet);\n} catch (err) {\n  logger.error({ err, passId: pass.id }, 'Failed to mint pass');\n  throw new MintPassError(pass.id, err);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. Testing Strategy\n\n1. Domain & Use-Cases – pure unit tests (Jest).  \n2. Contract tests for Ports (pact-style mocks).  \n3. Solidity contracts – Foundry + Hardhat coverage > 95 %.  \n4. End-to-End – Playwright + Ganache snapshotting.\n\n```ts\n// tests/use-cases/mintShowPass.spec.ts\ndescribe('MintShowPass', () => {\n  it('mints and publishes event', async () => {\n    const result = await useCase.execute({ showId, userWallet });\n    expect(result.passId).toBeDefined();\n    expect(eventSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ type: 'PassMinted' }));\n  });\n});\n```\n\n---\n\n## 11. Security Blueprint\n\n* All off-chain messages signed via EIP-712.  \n* On-chain re-entrancy guarded.  \n* Role-based access (`DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE`, `MINTER_ROLE`).  \n* Continuous audit pipeline with Slither + MythX.\n\n---\n\n## 12. Deployment Matrix\n\n| Env | Chain | Storage | Endpoint                  |\n|-----|-------|---------|---------------------------|\n| dev | Hardhat | local IPFS | http://localhost:4000 |\n| staging | Polygon Mumbai | Pinata | https://staging.api.stellar | \n| prod | Ethereum Mainnet | Filecoin | https://api.stellar |\n\n---\n\n> The architecture is designed to remain chain-agnostic: swapping Ethereum for Solana is merely a matter of implementing the same Ports in a new Adapter without touching business rules.\n\n_This concludes the blueprint._","numLines":347,"startLine":1,"totalLines":347}}}

Upgrade Workflow\n   115→\n   116→1. **Develop** new implementation in a feature branch.  \n   117→2. **Unit & drive tests** (`pnpm test:contracts`).  \n   118→3. **Propose upgrade** (generates calldata & JSON diff):\n   119→\n   120→```bash\n   121→pnpm ts-node scripts/upgrades/propose-upgrade.ts \\\n   122→  --proxy 0xProxyAddress \\\n   123→  --impl  contracts/pass/implementations/ShowPassV2.sol\n   124→```\n   125→\n   126→4. **Governor review** – core devs & community vote on chain.  \n   127→5. **Execute upgrade** (TimeLock enforced):\n   128→\n   129→```bash\n   130→pnpm ts-node scripts/upgrades/execute-upgrade.ts --proposal 123\n   131→```\n   132→\n   133→6. **Post-upgrade smoke test**: read `version()` & run snapshot tests.\n   134→\n   135→---\n   136→\n   137→## 6. Appendix – Sample Rollback Script\n   214→\n   215→```ts\n   216→// rollback.ts\n   217→import { ethers } from \"hardhat\";\n   218→import { TransparentUpgradeableProxy__factory } from \"../typechain\";\n   219→\n   220→async function main() {\n   221→  const proxyAddr = process.argv[2];\n   222→  const prevImpl  = process.argv[3];\n   223→  if (!proxyAddr || !prevImpl) throw new Error(\"Usage: rollback <proxy> <prevImpl>\");\n   224→\n   225→  const admin = await ethers.getSigner(\"multisig-emergency\");\n   226→  const proxy = TransparentUpgradeableProxy__factory.connect(proxyAddr, admin);\n   227→\n   228→  const tx = await proxy.upgradeTo(prevImpl);\n   229→  await tx.wait();\n   230→  console.log(`Rolled back ${proxyAddr} to ${prevImpl}`);\n   231→}\n   232→\n   233→main().catch(console.error);\n   234→```\n   235→\n   236→---\n   237→\n   238→Happy building, and may your stages remain stellar!  \n   239→– _Carnival Core Devs_\n   240→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. To support this pace **without forcing holders to burn & remint their NFTs**, every on-chain component is designed to be _upgradeable_ via the **Transparent Proxy pattern** (OpenZeppelin’s implementation) and protected by rigorous governance and testing gates.\n\nThis document is the single source of truth for:\n* Contract layout & storage guidelines  \n* Safe upgrade workflow (local → testnet → mainnet)  \n* Governance & emergency protocols  \n* Hardhat + TypeChain scripts and examples  \n\n---\n\n## 1. Architectural Overview\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph Proxy Layer\n        Proxy(Transparent\\nProxy)\n    end\n    subgraph Logic Layer\n        V1(ShowPassV1.sol)\n        V2(ShowPassV2.sol)\n    end\n    Proxy --delegatecall--> V1\n    Proxy --upgradeTo--> V2\n```\n\n* **Proxy Layer** (immutable): Stores state; delegates calls to the active logic contract.  \n* **Logic Layer** (upgradeable): ShowPassV1, V2, etc. Houses business rules only.  \n* **Admin Layer** (governed by `CarnivalGovernor`): Owns the proxy and controls `upgradeTo`.\n\n> NOTE: Upgrades never migrate data—the `Proxy` keeps the same storage slots across versions.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Directory Conventions\n\n```\ncontracts/\n  proxy/\n    BaseProxy.sol\n  pass/\n    implementations/\n      ShowPassV1.sol\n      ShowPassV2.sol\n    facets/          # For Diamond-style experiments\nscripts/\n  upgrades/\n    propose-upgrade.ts\n    approve-upgrade.ts\n    execute-upgrade.ts\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Writing an Upgradeable Contract\n\n### 3.1. Solidity Rules of Thumb\n1. **No constructors** – use `initialize()` instead.  \n2. **Never use `selfdestruct`** – breaks the proxy.  \n3. **Constants/APIs** – can be added, **never removed or reordered**.  \n4. **Storage gap** – reserve `uint256[50] private __gap;` for future vars.\n\n### 3.2. Example – `ShowPassV1.sol`\n\n```solidity\n// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.20;\n\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/token/ERC721/extensions/ERC721URIStorageUpgradeable.sol\";\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/proxy/utils/UUPSUpgradeable.sol\";\nimport \"../proxy/BaseProxy.sol\";\n\ncontract ShowPassV1 is ERC721URIStorageUpgradeable, UUPSUpgradeable {\n    /* ============ State ============ */\n    mapping(uint256 => uint256) private _exp; // Fan XP per pass\n\n    /* ============ Initializer ============ */\n    function initialize(string memory name_, string memory symbol_) public initializer {\n        __ERC721_init(name_, symbol_);\n        __UUPSUpgradeable_init();\n    }\n\n    /* ============ Core Logic ============ */\n    function gainExp(uint256 tokenId, uint256 amt) external {\n        require(_exists(tokenId), \"PASS_NOT_FOUND\");\n        _exp[tokenId] += amt;\n    }\n\n    /* ============ Upgradability ============ */\n    function _authorizeUpgrade(address newImpl) internal override onlyProxyAdmin {}\n    \n    uint256[49] private __gap; // 49 because we used one slot for _exp\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Storage Layout & Compatibility\n\nRun `npx hardhat storage:layout` before and after every change.  \nBreaking changes (detected via `@openzeppelin/upgrades-core`) will **halt the CI**.\n\nCommon pitfalls:\n* Re-ordering state variables  \n* Changing inheritances that inject storage (`Ownable`, `Pausable`)  \n* Converting `uint8` → `uint256` (size mismatch)\n\n---\n\n## 5. Upgrade Workflow\n\n1. **Develop** new implementation in a feature branch.  \n2. **Unit & drive tests** (`pnpm test:contracts`).  \n3. **Propose upgrade** (generates calldata & JSON diff):\n\n```bash\npnpm ts-node scripts/upgrades/propose-upgrade.ts \\\n  --proxy 0xProxyAddress \\\n  --impl  contracts/pass/implementations/ShowPassV2.sol\n```\n\n4. **Governor review** – core devs & community vote on chain.  \n5. **Execute upgrade** (TimeLock enforced):\n\n```bash\npnpm ts-node scripts/upgrades/execute-upgrade.ts --proposal 123\n```\n\n6. **Post-upgrade smoke test**: read `version()` & run snapshot tests.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Governance & Emergency\n\n* `CarnivalGovernor` (ERC-5805) owns the proxy admin role.  \n* Emergency multisig (5/9) can trigger `_rollback()` if:\n  * Severe bug endangering funds\n  * DAO vote unreachable within 12h\n\n---\n\n## 7. Hardhat Helper Scripts\n\n### 7.1. `propose-upgrade.ts`\n\n```ts\nimport { ethers, upgrades } from \"hardhat\";\nimport fs from \"node:fs/promises\";\n\nasync function main() {\n  const proxy = \"0xProxyAddress\";\n  const NewImpl = await ethers.getContractFactory(\"ShowPassV2\");\n  const proposal = await upgrades.prepareUpgrade(proxy, NewImpl);\n  await fs.writeFile(\n    `./scripts/upgrades/proposals/${Date.now()}.json`,\n    JSON.stringify({ proxy, proposal }, null, 2)\n  );\n  console.log(\"Upgrade prepared at:\", proposal);\n}\n\nmain().catch((e) => {\n  console.error(e);\n  process.exit(1);\n});\n```\n\n### 7.2. `execute-upgrade.ts`\n\n```ts\nimport { ethers, upgrades } from \"hardhat\";\nimport proposal from \"./proposals/1685381110.json\";\n\nasync function main() {\n  const admin = await ethers.getSigner(\"upgrade-admin\");\n  await upgrades.upgradeProxy(proposal.proxy, proposal.proposal, { signer: admin });\n  console.log(\"Proxy upgraded!\");\n}\n\nmain().catch((e) => {\n  console.error(e);\n  process.exit(1);\n});\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Test Matrix\n\n| Layer | Tooling                     | Responsibility                       |\n|-------|-----------------------------|--------------------------------------|\n| Unit  | Hardhat + viem             | Business invariants (XP, rarity)     |\n| Integration | Anvil fork + Foundry | Cross-contract interactions          |\n| E2E   | Hardhat Network + Mocha    | Upgrade, rollback, governance flow   |\n\nCI runs on every push; mainnet deploy uses the same tagged Docker image.\n\n---\n\n## 9. FAQ\n\n**Q: Can we migrate to a Diamond (EIP-2535) later?**  \nA: Yes—the proxy can delegate to a DiamondCutFacet in V3. Storage layout care is mandatory.\n\n**Q: What if an upgrade fails mid-transaction?**  \nA: The upgrade is atomic; either `upgradeTo` succeeds or the proxy keeps the old impl.\n\n---\n\n## 10. Appendix – Sample Rollback Script\n\n```ts\n// rollback.ts\nimport { ethers } from \"hardhat\";\nimport { TransparentUpgradeableProxy__factory } from \"../typechain\";\n\nasync function main() {\n  const proxyAddr = process.argv[2];\n  const prevImpl  = process.argv[3];\n  if (!proxyAddr || !prevImpl) throw new Error(\"Usage: rollback <proxy> <prevImpl>\");\n\n  const admin = await ethers.getSigner(\"multisig-emergency\");\n  const proxy = TransparentUpgradeableProxy__factory.connect(proxyAddr, admin);\n\n  const tx = await proxy.upgradeTo(prevImpl);\n  await tx.wait();\n  console.log(`Rolled back ${proxyAddr} to ${prevImpl}`);\n}\n\nmain().catch(console.error);\n```\n\n---\n\nHappy building, and may your stages remain stellar!  \n– _Carnival Core Devs_\n```","numLines":240,"startLine":1,"totalLines":240}}}

Event Subscriptions\n   191→```ts\n   192→// src/subscriptions/showPassEvents.ts\n   193→import { Contract } from 'ethers';\n   194→import { provider } from '@/lib/blockchain';\n   195→import ShowPassABI from '@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n   196→\n   197→const SHOW_PASS = '0x1cA5…BEEF';\n   198→\n   199→export function subscribeToPassEvolution(cb: (tokenId: bigint, level: number) => void) {\n   200→  const pass = new Contract(SHOW_PASS, ShowPassABI, provider);\n   201→  pass.on('LevelUp', (tokenId, level, event) => {\n   202→    console.debug('Pass evolved:', { tokenId, level, tx: event.transactionHash });\n   203→    cb(tokenId, Number(level));\n   204→  });\n   205→  return () => pass.removeAllListeners('LevelUp');\n   206→}\n   207→```\n   208→\n   209→---\n   210→\n   211→## 7. Error Handling Patterns\n   212→1. Always wrap read calls inside `try/catch` to avoid crashing renders.\n   213→2. Decode revert reasons for on-chain failures:\n   214→```ts\n   215→import { getRpcError } from 'ethers';\n   216→\n   217→try {\n   218→  await contract.doSomething();\n   219→} catch (e) {\n   220→  const reason = getRpcError(e)?.info?.error?.message ?? 'Unknown error';\n   221→  toast.error(`Tx failed: ${reason}`);\n   222→}\n   223→```\n   224→3. Apply exponential back-off when polling confirmations in flaky networks.\n   225→\n   226→---\n   227→\n   228→## 8. Gas & Fee Optimisations\n   229→• Prefer `staticCall` (aka call-static) to simulate transactions off-chain.  \n   230→• Batch multiple mints with `multicall(aggregate)` via `CarnivalMulticaller`.  \n   231→• Use EIP-1559 params: `maxFeePerGas`, `maxPriorityFeePerGas`.  \n   232→• Monitor `baseFee` with `provider.getFeeData()` and queue non-urgent jobs.\n   233→\n   234→---\n   235→\n   236→## 9. Advanced: Meta-Tx & Permit2\n   237→`ShowPassProxy` implements ERC-2771 recipient; you can pay gas on behalf of users.\n   238→\n   239→```ts\n   240→import { signTypedData } from '@wagmi/core'; // or viem\n   241→// …generate typedData object…\n   242→const signature = await signTypedData({ domain, types, value });\n   243→\n   244→await carnivalForwarder.execute({\n   245→  target: SHOW_PASS,\n   246→  data: pass.interface.encodeFunctionData('levelUp', [tokenId, traitHash]),\n   247→  signature\n   248→});\n   249→```\n   250→\n   251→---\n   252→\n   253→## 10. Reference Utilities\n   254→```ts\n   255→// src/utils/retry.ts\n   256→export async function retry<T>(\n   257→  fn: () => Promise<T>,\n   258→  attempts = 3,\n   259→  delayMs = 1_000\n   260→): Promise<T> {\n   261→  try {\n   262→    return await fn();\n   263→  } catch (err) {\n   264→    if (attempts <= 1) throw err;\n   265→    await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, delayMs));\n   266→    return retry(fn, attempts - 1, delayMs * 2);\n   267→  }\n   268→}\n   269→```\n   270→\n   271→---\n   272→\n   273→Happy building, and see you on the Stellar Stage! 🚀\n   274→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Stateful Transactions\n### 5.1 Mint a ShowPass\n```ts\n// src/tx/mintShowPass.ts\nimport { Contract } from 'ethers';\nimport { signer } from '@/lib/blockchain';\nimport ShowPassABI from tx = await escrow.lock(tokenId, lockUntil);\n  console.info(`Staking tx ➜ ${tx.hash}`);\n  return tx.wait();\n}\n```\n\n### 5.3 Cast a Live Vote\n```ts\n// src/tx/castLiveVote.ts\nimport { Contract } from 'ethers';\nimport { signer } from '@/lib/blockchain';\nimport GovernorABI from '@/contracts/abi/CarnivalGovernor.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n\nconst GOVERNOR = '0xFabC…A11A';\n\nexport async function castLiveVote(proposalId: bigint, support: 0 | 1 | 2) {\n  const governor = new Contract(GOVERNOR, GovernorABI, signer);\n\n  const params = await governor.proposalSnapshot(proposalId);\n  if (BigInt(Date.now() / 1000) < params.startTime) {\n    throw new Error('Voting has not started yet.');\n  }\n\n  const tx = await governor.castVote(proposalId, support);\n  console.info(`Vote tx ➜ ${tx.hash}`);\n  return tx.wait();\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Event Subscriptions\n```ts\n// src/subscriptions/showPassEvents.ts\nimport { Contract } from 'ethers';\nimport { provider } from '@/lib/blockchain';\nimport ShowPassABI from '@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n\nconst SHOW_PASS = '0x1cA5…BEEF';\n\nexport function subscribeToPassEvolution(cb: (tokenId: bigint, level: number) => void) {\n  const pass = new Contract(SHOW_PASS, ShowPassABI, provider);\n  pass.on('LevelUp', (tokenId, level, event) => {\n    console.debug('Pass evolved:', { tokenId, level, tx: event.transactionHash });\n    cb(tokenId, Number(level));\n  });\n  return () => pass.removeAllListeners('LevelUp');\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Error Handling Patterns\n1. Always wrap read calls inside `try/catch` to avoid crashing renders.\n2. Decode revert reasons for on-chain failures:\n```ts\nimport { getRpcError } from 'ethers';\n\ntry {\n  await contract.doSomething();\n} catch (e) {\n  const reason = getRpcError(e)?.info?.error?.message ?? 'Unknown error';\n  toast.error(`Tx failed: ${reason}`);\n}\n```\n3. Apply exponential back-off when polling confirmations in flaky networks.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Gas & Fee Optimisations\n• Prefer `staticCall` (aka call-static) to simulate transactions off-chain.  \n• Batch multiple mints with `multicall(aggregate)` via `CarnivalMulticaller`.  \n• Use EIP-1559 params: `maxFeePerGas`, `maxPriorityFeePerGas`.  \n• Monitor `baseFee` with `provider.getFeeData()` and queue non-urgent jobs.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Advanced: Meta-Tx & Permit2\n`ShowPassProxy` implements ERC-2771 recipient; you can pay gas on behalf of users.\n\n```ts\nimport { signTypedData } from '@wagmi/core'; // or viem\n// …generate typedData object…\nconst signature = await signTypedData({ domain, types, value });\n\nawait carnivalForwarder.execute({\n  target: SHOW_PASS,\n  data: pass.interface.encodeFunctionData('levelUp', [tokenId, traitHash]),\n  signature\n});\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. Reference Utilities\n```ts\n// src/utils/retry.ts\nexport async function retry<T>(\n  fn: () => Promise<T>,\n  attempts = 3,\n  delayMs = 1_000\n): Promise<T> {\n  try {\n    return await fn();\n  } catch (err) {\n    if (attempts <= 1) throw err;\n    await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, delayMs));\n    return retry(fn, attempts - 1, delayMs * 2);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\nHappy building, and see you on the Stellar Stage! 🚀\n```","numLines":274,"startLine":1,"totalLines":274}}}

UpgradeProposed(msg.sender, newImplementation, executeAfter);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Executes a previously proposed upgrade once timelock expired.\n     * @param newImplementation Address of the new logic contract (must match\n     *                           the proposal).\n     */\n    function upgradeTo(address newImplementation) public onlyAdmin {\n        _checkTimelock(newImplementation);\n        _upgradeTo(newImplementation);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Same as `upgradeTo` but also calls a function on the new\n     *         implementation (e.g. `initializeV2`).\n     */\n    function upgradeToAndCall(address newImplementation, bytes calldata data) external payable onlyAdmin {\n        _checkTimelock(newImplementation);\n        _upgradeTo(newImplementation);\n\n        if (data.length > 0) {\n            _functionDelegateCall(newImplementation, data);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                        ADMIN MAINTENANCE UTILITIES                    */\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Transfers proxy admin rights.\n     */\n    function changeAdmin(address newAdmin) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(newAdmin != address(0), \"Proxy: new admin zero\");\n\n        emit AdminChanged(admin(), newAdmin);\n        _setAdmin(newAdmin);\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                          FALLBACK / RECEIVE                           */\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    fallback() external payable virtual {\n        _delegate(implementation());\n    }\n\n    receive() external payable virtual {\n        _delegate(implementation());\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                       INTERNAL LOW-LEVEL HELPERS                      */\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    function _delegate(address impl) internal virtual {\n        assembly {\n            // Copy msg.data. We take full control of memory in this inline\n            // assembly block because it will not return to Solidity code. We\n            // overwrite the Solidity scratch pad at memory position 0.\n            calldatacopy(0, 0, calldatasize())\n\n            // Delegatecall to the implementation.\n            let result := delegatecall(gas(), impl, 0, calldatasize(), 0, 0)\n\n            // Copy the returned data.\n            returndatacopy(0, 0, returndatasize())\n\n            switch result\n                case 0 {\n                    // Delegatecall failed.\n                    revert(0, returndatasize())\n                }\n                default {\n                    // Delegatecall succeeded.\n                    return(0, returndatasize())\n                }\n        }\n    }\n\n    function _upgradeTo(address newImplementation) internal {\n        _setImplementation(newImplementation);\n        // Reset proposal/timelock\n        _setUpgradeProposal(address(0));\n        _setTimelock(0);\n\n        emit Upgraded(msg.sender, newImplementation);\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                             STORAGE SETTERS                           */\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    function _setAdmin(address newAdmin) private {\n        assembly {\n            sstore(_ADMIN_SLOT, newAdmin)\n        }\n    }\n\n    function _setImplementation(address newImplementation) private {\n        assembly {\n            sstore(_IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT, newImplementation)\n        }\n    }\n\n    function _setUpgradeProposal(address proposal) private {\n        assembly {\n            sstore(_UPGRADE_PROPOSAL_SLOT, proposal)\n        }\n    }\n\n    function _setTimelock(uint256 unlockTime) private {\n        assembly {\n            sstore(_TIMELOCK_SLOT, unlockTime)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                          VALIDATION UTILITIES                         */\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    function _checkTimelock(address expectedImplementation) private view {\n        address proposal;\n        uint256 unlockTime;\n\n        assembly {\n            proposal := sload(_UPGRADE_PROPOSAL_SLOT)\n            unlockTime := sload(_TIMELOCK_SLOT)\n        }\n\n        require(proposal == expectedImplementation, \"Proxy: proposal mismatch\");\n        require(unlockTime != 0, \"Proxy: no timelock set\");\n        require(block.timestamp >= unlockTime, \"Proxy: timelock active\");\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Performs a solidity-style delegatecall.\n     */\n    function _functionDelegateCall(address target, bytes memory data) private {\n        require(target.isContract(), \"Proxy: delegate to non-contract\");\n\n        (bool success, bytes memory returndata) = target.delegatecall(data);\n        Address.verifyCallResult(success, returndata, \"Proxy: delegate call failed\");\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                               MODIFIERS                               */\n    /* --------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    modifier onlyAdmin() {\n        require(msg.sender == admin(), \"Proxy: caller not admin\");\n        _;\n    }\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                           Address Utility ( OZ )                          */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * @dev Minimal subset of OpenZeppelin’s Address library for inline use.\n */\nlibrary Address {\n    function isContract(address account) internal view returns (bool) {\n        return account.code.length > 0;\n    }\n\n    function verifyCallResult(\n        bool success,\n        bytes memory returndata,\n        string memory errorMessage\n    ) internal pure {\n        if (success) return;\n        // Look for revert reason and bubble it up if present\n        if (returndata.length > 0) {\n            // The easiest way to bubble the revert reason is using memory via\n            // assembly\n            assembly {\n                let returndata_size := mload(returndata)\n                revert(add(32, returndata), returndata_size)\n            }\n        } else {\n            revert(errorMessage);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":310,"startLine":1,"totalLines":310}}}

    return await response.wait(confirmations);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Wait for an already-submitted tx-hash\n     */\n    public async waitForTx(txHash: string, confirmations = !tx.maxPriorityFeePerGas)) {\n            const feeData = await this.provider.getFeeData();\n            if (feeData.maxFeePerGas && feeData.maxPriorityFeePerGas) {\n                // Prefer EIP-1559 (await this.signer.getTransactionCount('pending'));\n\n        return tx;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Estimate gas w/ buffer to reduce out-of‐gas (defaults to +20 %)\n     */\n    public async estimateGas(\n        tx: providers.TransactionRequest | Contract\n    ): Promise<ethers.BigNumber> {\n        let estimate: ethers.BigNumber;\n        if (tx instanceof Contract) {\n            throw new Error('Cannot estimate gas for raw contract; call method estimateGas.*');\n        } else {\n            estimate = await this.provider.estimateGas(tx);\n        }\n\n        return estimate\n            .mul(Math.round(this.options.gasLimitBuffer * 100))\n            .div(100);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                       Contract deployment / proxies                    */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /**\n     * Deploy an implementation contract and wrap it with an OpenZeppelin Transparent\n     * Upgradeable Proxy.  Returns a contract instance that is already connected to the\n     * signer and uses the implementation’s ABI.  Only *very* light-weight: for truly\n     * complex deployments use Hardhat upgrades-plugin in scripts.\n     */\n    public async deployProxy(\n        implementationArtifact: ContractArtifact,\n        initializeArgs: readonly any[] = []\n    ): Promise<Contract> {\n        // 1) Deploy implementation\n        const implFactory = new ContractFactory(\n            implementationArtifact.abi,\n            implementationArtifact.bytecode,\n            this.signer\n        );\n\n        const implContract = await implFactory.deploy();\n        await implContract.deployed();\n\n        // 2) Encode initializer data\n        const initCalldata = implContract.interface.encodeFunctionData(\n            'initialize',\n            initializeArgs\n        );\n\n        // 3) Deploy proxy (using OpenZeppelin factory artefact shipped with NPM)\n        //    We inline ABI for tiny footprint, bytecode pulled from env/artefacts.\n        const PROXY_ABI = [\n            'constructor(address _logic,address admin,bytes memory _data)',\n            'function implementation() public view returns (address)'\n        ];\n        const PROXY_BYTECODE =\n            process.env.OZ_TRANSPARENT_PROXY_BYTECODE || // optionally injected at build-time\n            '0x'; // ⚠ placeholder – should be replaced with compiled bytecode\n\n        assert.ok(\n            PROXY_BYTECODE !== '0x',\n            'TransparentUpgradeableProxy bytecode missing. Contract(proxy.address, implementationArtifact.abi, this.signer);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*              */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Parse a VM revert reason (EIP-838).  ethers v5 already decodes many errors but\n * sometimes we need manual fall-back for old nodes.\n */\nexport function parseRevertReason(error: any): string | null {\n    try {\n        if (error?.data?.message) return error.data.message;\n It lives purely\n * in the domain layer and knows nothing about how/where it will be persisted\n * or minted on-chain.  All side-effects are exposed via Domain Events so that\n * application services and infrastructure adapters can react appropriately.\n */\n\nimport { v4 as uuid } from 'uuid';\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                              Utility / Helpers                             */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ntype EthereumAddress = `0x${string}`;\n\n/**\n * Very small address validator.  In a real code-base we might depend on\n * `ethers.utils.isAddress`, but the domain layer avoids heavy libs.\n */\nfunction assertValidAddress(address: string): asserts address is EthereumAddress {\n  const re = /^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$/;\n  if (!re.test(address)) {\n    throw new DomainError(`Invalid Ethereum address: ${address}`);\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                  Errors                                    */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport class DomainError extends Error {\n  readonly name = 'DomainError';\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Domain Events                                */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport interface DomainEvent<T = unknown> {\n  readonly name: string;\n  readonly payload: T;\n  readonly occurredOn: Date;\n}\n\n/**\n * A super-lightweight synchronous event dispatcher used only in the domain\n * layer to register side-effect-free callbacks. Infrastructure code can wire\n * this up to a real bus (RabbitMQ, SNS, etc.) at the application boundary.\n */\nexport class DomainEvents {\n  private static handlers: Map<string, Set<(event: DomainEvent) => void>> =\n    new Map();\n\n  static publish(event: DomainEvent): void {\n    const listeners = this.handlers.get(event.name);\n    if (!listeners?.size) return;\n\n    listeners.forEach((cb) => {\n      try {\n        cb(event);\n      } catch (err) {\n        /* eslint-disable no-console */\n        console.error(`[DomainEvents] Handler threw for event ${event.name}`, err);\n        /* eslint-enable  no-console */\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  static subscribe(eventName: string, cb: (event: DomainEvent) => void): void {\n    const listeners = this.handlers.get(eventName) ?? new Set();\n    listeners.add(cb);\n    this.handlers.set(eventName, listeners);\n  }\n\n  static clear(): void {\n    this.handlers.clear();\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Value Objects                                */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/** A trait is a mutable property embedded in token metadata. */\nexport interface PassTrait {\n  key: string;\n  value: string | number | boolean;\n  /** RFC-3339 ISO string; easier to serialize than Date. */\n  lastUpdated: string;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                Aggregate                                   */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ninterface ShowPassProps {\n  tokenId: bigint; // On-chain tokenId (ERC-721 compatible).\n  showId: string; // UUID of the Show aggregate this pass belongs to.\n  owner: EthereumAddress;\n  traits: PassTrait[];\n  /** Unix millis where the pass began staking. 0 means not currently staked. */\n  stakedAt: number;\n  /** Total governance voting power accumulated by this pass. */\n  governancePower: number;\n  /** URI pointing to the IPFS JSON metadata. */\n  metadataUri: string;\n  /** Logical deletion flag. Does NOT perform on-chain transfer—that is handled\n   * elsewhere. * File: mint-show-pass.use-case.ts\n * Project: StellarStage Carnival – Interactive NFT Showrunner\n * Layer: Application ‑ Use-Cases\n *\n * Description:\n *   Executes the business flow required to mint a new Show-Pass NFT for a given show.\n *   The use-case coordinates the domain factory, repositories, external ports (EVM,\n *   IPFS, Event-Bus) and returns the newly minted Pass aggregate root.\n *\n *   Clean Architecture rules:\n *     • Knows nothing about HTTP, CLI, GraphQL, etc.\n *     • Orchestrates domain & infrastructure by calling abstractions (ports).\n *     • Produces domain errors instead of infrastructure-specific ones.\n *\n * Author: StellarStage Carnival Core Team\n ****************************************************************************************/\n\nimport { v4 as uuid } from 'uuid';\nimport { Pass } from '../../../domain/entities/pass.entity';\nimport { Show } from '../../../domain/entities/show.entity';\nimport { PassTier } from '../../../domain/value-objects/pass-tier.vo';\nimport { PassFactory } from '../../../domain/factories/pass.factory';\nimport { IPassRepository } from '../../../domain/ports/repositories/pass.repository.port';\nimport { IShowRepository } from '../../../domain/ports/repositories/show.repository.port';\nimport { IBlockchainPort } from '../../../domain/ports/blockchain.port';\nimport { IStoragePort } from '../../../domain/ports/storage.port';\nimport { IEventBusPort } from '../../../domain/ports/event-bus.port';\nimport { DomainError } from '../../../shared/errors/domain.error';\nimport { Either, left,        */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport interface MintShowPassCommand {\n  showId: string;\n  ownerWallet: string;\n  tier: PassTier;\n  metadataOverrides?: Record<string, unknown>;\n  txOptions?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Use-Case Class                               */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport class MintShowPassUseCase {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly showRepo: IShowRepository,\n    private readonly passRepo: IPassRepository,\n    private readonly blockchain: IBlockchainPort,\n    private readonly storage: IStoragePort,\n    private readonly eventBus: IEventBusPort\n  ) {}\n\n  /**\n   * Main entry – orchestrates the entire mint workflow.\n   */\n  public async execute(\n    command: MintShowPassCommand\n  ): Promise<Either<DomainError, Pass>> {\n    /* ------------------------------- Validation -------------------------- */\n    const validationError = this.validateCommand(command);\n    if (validationError) return left(validationError);\n\n    /* ----------------------------- Load Show ----------------------------- */\n    const show: Show | undefined = await this.showRepo.findById(command.showId);\n    if (!show)\n      return left(\n        new DomainError(\n          'SHOW_NOT_FOUND',\n          `Show with id \"${command.showId}\" does not exist`\n        )\n      );\n\n    /* --------------------------- Factory Create -------------------------- */\n    let pass: Pass;\n    try {\n      pass = PassFactory.create({\n        id: uuid(),\n        showId: show.id,\n        ownerWallet: command.ownerWallet,\n        tier: command.tier,\n        metadataOverrides: command.metadataOverrides\n      });\n    } catch (err) {\n      return left(\n        new DomainError('PASS_CREATION_FAILED', (err as Error).message)\n      );\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------- Persist to DB --------------------------- */\n    try {\n      await this.passRepo.save(pass);\n    } catch (err) {\n      return left(\n        new DomainError('PERSISTENCE_ERROR', 'Could not persist new pass')\n      );\n    }\n\n    /* ----------------------- Pin metadata to IPFS ------------------------ */\n    try {\n      const ipfsHash = await this.storage.pinJSON(pass.toMetadataJSON());\n      pass.setTokenURI(`ipfs://${ipfsHash}`);\n      await this.passRepo.update(pass);\n    } catch (err) {\n      return left(\n        new DomainError(\n          'STORAGE_ERROR',\n          'IPFS pinning failed: ' + (err as Error).message\n        )\n      );\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------ Smart-Contract / Blockchain ---------------------- */\n    try {\n      const txHash = await this.blockchain.mintNFT({\n        to: pass.ownerWallet,\n        tokenURI: pass.tokenURI,\n        tier: pass.tier,\n        txOptions: command.txOptions\n      });\n\n      pass.setMintTxHash(txHash);\n      await this.passRepo.update(pass);\n    } catch (err) {\n      // Rollback DB on chain failure\n      await this.passRepo.delete(pass.id).catch(() => void 0);\n      return left(\n        new DomainError(\n          'BLOCKCHAIN_ERROR',\n          'Mint transaction failed: ' + (err as Error).message\n        )\n      );\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------- Domain Events --------------------------- */\n    await this.eventBus.publish('pass.minted', {\n      passId: pass.id,\n      showId: pass.showId,\n      owner: pass.ownerWallet,\n      tier: pass.tier\n    });\n\n    return right(pass);\n  }\n\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n  /*                               Utilities                                */\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n  private validateCommand(command: MintShowPassCommand): DomainError | null {\n    if (!command.showId) {\n      return new DomainError('VALIDATION_ERROR', 'showId is required');\n    }\n    if (!command.ownerWallet) {\n      return new DomainError('VALIDATION_ERROR', 'ownerWallet is required');\n    }\n    if (!PassTier.isValid(command.tier)) {\n      return new DomainError(\n        'VALIDATION_ERROR',\n        `tier \"${command.tier}\" is invalid`\n      );\n    }\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n```\n","numLines":171,"startLine":1,"totalLines":171}}}

Used to validate provenance. */\n  performer?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface UpgradePassByEventResult {\n  passId: string;\n  newLevel: number;\n  /** Transaction hash of the on-chain metadata update. */\n  txHash: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Application-layer use-case that upgrades a Pass NFT when an in-show event\n * qualifies for a trait evolution.\n *\n * The class follows the Clean Architecture rule of having no dependencies on\n * framework code and relies solely on ports for I/O.\n */\nexport class UpgradePassByEventUseCase {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly passRepository: PassRepositoryPort,\n    private readonly eventRepository: ShowEventRepositoryPort,\n    private readonly traitFactory: TraitFactoryPort,\n    private readonly nftGateway: NFTGatewayPort,\n    private readonly eventBus: EventBusPort,\n    private readonly logger: Logger\n  ) {}\n\n  /**\n   * Executes the upgrade business flow.\n   */\n  public async execute(\n    command: UpgradePassByEventCommand\n  ): Promise<UpgradePassByEventResult> {\n    this.logger.debug(\n      `[upgrade-pass] Received command: pass=${command.passId}, event=${command.eventId}`\n    );\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /* 1. Fetch aggregates                                                    */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    const [pass, event] = await Promise.all([\n      this.passRepository.findById(command.passId),\n      this.eventRepository.findById(command.eventId)\n    ]);\n\n    if (!pass) {\n      this.logger.warn(`[upgrade-pass] Pass ${command.passId} not found`);\n      throw new DomainError('PASS_NOT_FOUND', 'Pass does not exist');\n    }\n\n    if (!event) {\n      this.logger.warn(`[upgrade-pass] Event ${command.eventId} not found`);\n      throw new DomainError('EVENT_NOT_FOUND', 'Event does not exist');\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /* 2. Domain validation                                                   */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    if (!event.qualifiesForUpgrade()) {\n      throw new DomainError(\n        'EVENT_NOT_ELIGIBLE',\n        `Event ${event.id} does not qualify for upgrades`\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (pass.isOnCooldown()) {\n      throw new DomainError(\n        'PASS_ON_COOLDOWN',\n        `Pass is on cooldown until ${pass.cooldownEndsAt?.toISOString()}`\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (command.performer && command.performer !== event.performerAddress) {\n      throw new DomainError(\n        'PERFORMER_MISMATCH',\n        'Provided performer address does not match event details'\n      );\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /* 3. Trait generation                                                    */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    const traitComputation = await this.traitFactory.buildTraitsFromEvent({\n      pass,\n      event\n    });\n\n    if (!traitComputation.updated) {\n      // Nothing to upgrade; return early.\n      this.logger.info(`[upgrade-pass] No new traits for pass ${pass.id}`);\n      return {\n        passId: pass.id,\n        newLevel: pass.level,\n        txHash: ''\n      };\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /* 4. Mutate aggregate & persist                                          */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    pass.applyTraitUpdate(traitComputation);\n    pass.bumpLevel();\n    pass.markCooldown();\n\n    await this.passRepository.save(pass);\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /* 5. Push metadata on-chain                                              */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    let txHash = '';\n    try {\n      const input: UpdateTokenMetadataInput = {\n        tokenId: pass.tokenId,\n        metadata: pass.toMetadata()\n      };\n\n      txHash = await this.nftGateway.updateTokenMetadata(input);\n    } catch (error) {\n      this.logger.error(\n        `[upgrade-pass] Blockchain update failed for pass ${pass.id}. Rolling back.`,\n        error as Error\n      );\n      await this.passRepository.rollback(pass.id);\n      throw new DomainError(\n        'BLOCKCHAIN_TX_FAILED',\n        'Unable to broadcast metadata update to blockchain'\n      );\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /* 6. Publish domain event                                                */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    const domainEvent = new PassUpgradedDomainEvent({\n      passId: pass.id,\n      eventId: event.id,\n      newLevel: pass.level,\n      traits: pass.traits,\n      txHash\n    });\n\n    await this.eventBus.publish(domainEvent);\n\n    this.logger.info(\n      `[upgrade-pass] Pass ${pass.id} upgraded to level ${pass.level}. Tx=${txHash}`\n    );\n\n    return {\n      passId: pass.id,\n      newLevel: pass.level,\n      txHash\n    };\n  }\n}\n```","numLines":189,"startLine":1,"totalLines":189}}}

The analysis covers the upgrade mechanism, off-chain dependencies, deployment sequence, and risk mitigation strategies required to safely execute this critical infrastructure change.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Upgrade Mechanism Identification\n\n### 1.1 Pattern Analysis\n\nAfter thorough analysis of the codebase, the StellarStage Carnival platform uses a **hybrid proxy architecture** that combines elements of both **UUPS (Universal Upgradeable Proxy Standard)** and **Transparent Proxy** patterns:\n\n#### Implementation Contract (`contracts/core/ShowPass.sol`)\nThe core `ShowPass.sol` contract inherits from `UUPSUpgradeable` (line 26, 45-46):\n\n```solidity\nimport {UUPSUpgradeable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/proxy/utils/UUPSUpgradeable.sol\";\n\ncontract ShowPass is\n    ERC721EnumerableUpgradeable,\n    ERC2981Upgradeable,\n    AccessControlUpgradeable,\n    PausableUpgradeable,\n    ReentrancyGuardUpgradeable,\n    UUPSUpgradeable\n```\n\nThe upgrade authorization is controlled via the `_authorizeUpgrade` function (lines 287-291):\n\n```solidity\nfunction _authorizeUpgrade(address newImplementation)\n    internal\n    override\n    onlyRole(ADMIN_ROLE)\n{}\n```\n\n#### Proxy Contract (`contracts/proxies/ShowPassProxy.sol`)\nThe proxy contract is a **custom ERC-1967-compliant implementation** with additional governance features:\n- **48-hour timelock** for upgrades (`MIN_TIMELOCK = 48 hours`, line 55)\n- **Two-step upgrade flow**: `proposeUpgrade()` followed by `upgradeTo()` after timelock expiration\n- **EIP-1967 storage slots** for admin and implementation addresses\n\nKey storage slots (lines 39-52):\n```solidity\nbytes32 private constant _ADMIN_SLOT =\n    0xb53127684a568b3173ae13b9f8a6016e019ccc0d1d41b79e4b938e3c7e40d0ed;\n\nbytes32 private constant _IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT =\n    0x360894a13ba1a3210667c828492db98dca3e2076cc3735a920a3ca505d382bbc;\n```\n\n### 1.2 Upgrade Flow Summary\n\n1. **proxyAdmin** calls `proposeUpgrade(newImplementation)` on the proxy\n2. 48-hour timelock begins\n3. After timelock expiration, **proxyAdmin** calls `upgradeTo(newImplementation)` or `upgradeToAndCall(newImplementation, initData)` for initializer calls\n4. The proxy updates `_IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT` and clears the proposal/timelock state\n5. All future delegatecalls route to the new implementation\n\n---\n\n## 2. Off-Chain Dependencies Analysis\n\n### 2.1 Critical Backend Components\n\nThe following files in `packages/backend` are tightly coupled to the `ShowPass.sol` contract's ABI and storage layout:\n\n#### 2.1.1 `packages/backend/src/infrastructure/adapters/blockchain/ethers.service.ts`\n\n**Purpose:** Primary blockchain interaction layer that wraps ethers.js\n**Dependency Type:** Direct ABI dependency\n\n**Critical Functions (lines 283-289):**\n```typescript\npublic loadContract<T extends Contract = Contract>(\n    address: string,\n    abi: any,\n    signerOrProvider: Wallet | providers.Provider = this.signer\n): T {\n    return new Contract(address, abi, signerOrProvider) as T;\n}\n```\n\n**Why Dependent:**\n- Instantiates `ethers.Contract` objects using the ShowPass ABI\n- Event listeners registered via `on()` method (lines 295-305) will fail if event signatures change\n- Transaction encoding relies on ABI function selectors; any function signature change will cause transaction failures\n\n#### 2.1.2 `packages/backend/src/infrastructure/adapters/blockchain/contract.mapper.ts`\n\n**Purpose:** Maps domain entities to blockchain transactions and parses chain events\n**Dependency Type:** ABI encoding/decoding dependency\n\n**Critical Code (lines 52-68):**\n```typescript\nconst ABI_FRAGMENT: Record<\n  'ShowPass' | 'Loot' | 'Governance',\n  ContractInterface\n> = {\n  ShowPass: [\n    'function safeMint(address to, uint256 tokenId, string memory uri) public',\n    'event Transfer(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 indexed tokenId)',\n  ],\n  // ...\n};\n```\n\n**Why Dependent:**\n- Hard-coded ABI fragments for encoding `safeMint()` transactions (lines 125-151)\n- Event parsing in `toDomainEvent()` (lines 184-243) relies on specific event signatures\n- Adding new royalty-related events will require updates to the event mapping switch statement\n- Any function signature changes (e.g., adding tiered royalty parameters to `safeMint`) will break transaction encoding\n\n#### 2.1.3 `packages/backend/src/application/observers/stage-event.observer.ts`\n\n**Purpose:** Listens to domain events and broadcasts to external delivery mechanisms\n**Dependency Type:** Event schema dependency\n\n**Critical Code (lines 53-89):**\n```typescript\nasync on(event: DomainEvent): Promise<void> {\n  switch (event.constructor) {\n    case ActStartedEvent:\n      await this.handleActStarted(event as ActStartedEvent);\n      break;\n    case PassStakedEvent:\n      await this.handlePassStaked(event as PassStakedEvent);\n      break;\n    // ...\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Why Dependent:**\n- If the upgrade adds new events (e.g., `RoyaltyTierChanged`), this observer must be updated to handle them\n- WebSocket broadcasts to frontends depend on consistent event payloads\n- Failure to update will cause new events to be silently ignored (falls through to `default` case)\n\n#### 2.1.4 `packages/backend/src/domain/entities/show-pass.entity.ts`\n\n**Purpose:** Domain model representing the off-chain ShowPass entity\n**Dependency Type:** Data model alignment\n\n**Critical Properties (lines 100-117):**\n```typescript\ninterface ShowPassProps {\n  tokenId: bigint;\n  showId: string;\n  owner: EthereumAddress;\n  traits: PassTrait[];\n  stakedAt: number;\n  governancePower: number;\n  metadataUri: string;\n  burned: boolean;\n  createdAt: number;\n  updatedAt: number;\n}\n```\n\n**Why Dependent:**\n- Adding tiered royalties may require new domain properties (e.g., `royaltyTier`, `royaltyBasisPoints`)\n- The `toJSON()` serialization and `fromPersistence()` hydration methods must be updated\n- Domain events like `ShowPassMinted` (line 153-158) may need additional payload fields\n\n#### 2.1.5 Additional Dependent Files\n\n| File | Dependency Type | Impact |\n|------|-----------------|--------|\n| `packages/backend/src/application/use-cases/pass/mint-show-pass.use-case.ts` | Transaction flow | Must handle new royalty tier parameters during minting |\n| `packages/backend/src/application/use-cases/pass/upgrade-pass-by-event.use-case.ts` | Metadata updates | On-chain metadata push via `nftGateway.updateTokenMetadata()` |\n| `packages/backend/src/application/ports/IBlockchain.service.ts` | Interface definition | Port may need new method signatures for royalty operations |\n| `packages/backend/src/infrastructure/adapters/database/repositories/postgres-show-pass.repository.ts` | Persistence layer | Database schema must align with new royalty fields |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Upgrade Sequence (Step-by-Step)\n\n### 3.1 Pre-Deployment Phase (Off-Chain)\n\n| Step | Action | Responsible Team | Duration |\n|------|--------|-----------------|----------|\n| 1 | **Code Freeze** - Feature freeze on `ShowPass` related backend code | DevOps | 1 day |\n| 2 | **Storage Layout Verification** - Run `npx hardhat storage:layout` on both old and new contracts, compare using `@openzeppelin/upgrades-core` | Smart Contract Team | 2 hours |\n| 3 | **ABI Generation** - Compile new implementation, generate TypeChain types | Smart Contract Team | 1 hour |\n| 4 | **Backend ABI Update** - Update `ABI_FRAGMENT` in `contract.mapper.ts` with new royalty functions/events | Backend Team | 2 hours |\n| 5 | **Domain Model Update** - Add royalty tier properties to `show-pass.entity.ts` | Backend Team | 4 hours |\n| 6 | **Database Migration** - Create and test migration scripts for new columns | Backend Team | 4 hours |\n| 7 | **Observer Updates** - Add handlers for new events in `stage-event.observer.ts` | Backend Team | 2 hours |\n| 8 | **Integration Tests** - Run full test suite against forked mainnet with new implementation | QA Team | 1 day |\n| 9 | **Frontend Notification** - Prepare maintenance banner and user communications | Product Team | 1 day |\n\n### 3.2 On-Chain Deployment Phase\n\n| Step | Action | Command/Script | Duration |\n|------|--------|----------------|----------|\n| 1 | **Deploy New Implementation** | `npx ts-node scripts/upgrades/propose-upgrade.ts --proxy 0xProxyAddress --impl contracts/core/ShowPassV2.sol` | 5 min |\n| 2 | **Propose Upgrade** | Admin calls `ShowPassProxy.proposeUpgrade(newImplAddress)` | 5 min |\n| 3 | **Timelock Period** | **48 HOUR MANDATORY WAIT** - No action | 48 hours |\n| 4 | **Execute Upgrade** | Admin calls `ShowPassProxy.upgradeToAndCall(newImplAddress, initializeTieredRoyaltiesData)` | 5 min |\n| 5 | **Post-Upgrade Verification** | Run `npx hardhat proxy:impl --proxy-address 0xProxyAddress` to verify new implementation | 2 min |\n| 6 | **Smoke Tests** | Call `version()` and new royalty functions on upgraded proxy | 10 min |\n\n### 3.3 Off-Chain Synchronization Phase\n\n| Step | Action | Responsible Team | Duration |\n|------|--------|-----------------|----------|\n| 1 | **Environment Variables** | Update `SHOW_PASS_IMPL_ADDRESS` in backend `.env` (if tracked separately) | 5 min |\n| 2 | **Database Migration Execution** | Run `prisma migrate deploy` in production | 10 min |\n| 3 | **Backend Rolling Restart** | Deploy new backend version with updated ABI | 15 min |\n| 4 | **Cache Invalidation** | Clear any cached contract instances (Redis, in-memory) | 5 min |\n| 5 | **Event Listener Restart** | Restart `stage-event.observer.ts` to pick up new event subscriptions | 5 min |\n| 6 | **GraphQL Schema Update** | Deploy updated GraphQL schema with royalty fields | 10 min |\n| 7 | **Frontend Deployment** | Deploy frontend with new royalty tier UI components | 30 min |\n| 8 | **End-to-End Verification** | Full user flow testing in production | 30 min |\n\n### 3.4 Critical Ordering Requirements\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    A[1. Deploy New Implementation] --> B[2. Propose Upgrade on Proxy]\n    B --> C{48h Timelock}\n    C --> D[3. Execute Upgrade]\n    D --> E[4. Verify On-Chain]\n    E --> F[5. Run DB Migrations]\n    F --> G[6. Deploy Backend with New ABI]\n    G --> H[7. Restart Event Listeners]\n    H --> I[8. Deploy Frontend]\n    I --> J[9. E2E Verification]\n```\n\n**CRITICAL:** Steps 5-7 (database, backend, event listeners) MUST happen AFTER step 4 (execute upgrade) but BEFORE any user traffic is allowed. The backend MUST NOT call the new contract functions until the upgrade is complete, otherwise transactions will revert.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Risk Assessment and Mitigation\n\n### 4.1 Risk: Storage Layout Collision\n\n**Severity:** CRITICAL\n**Likelihood:** Medium (especially with complex inheritance chains)\n\n**Description:**\nThe `ShowPass.sol` contract inherits from 6 OpenZeppelin upgradeable contracts. Each adds storage variables at specific slots. Re-ordering state variables, changing inheritance order, or modifying variable types between V1 and V2 will corrupt proxy storage.\n\n**Affected Code (`ShowPass.sol`, lines 59-70):**\n```solidity\nstruct PassData {\n    uint8   level;\n    uint32  xp;\n    bool    staked;\n    uint64  stakeTimestamp;\n}\n\nCounters.Counter private _tokenIds;\nmapping(uint256 => PassData) internal _passData;\nstring private _baseTokenURI;\n```\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Automated Storage Verification:** Add CI step that runs OpenZeppelin's `upgradeProxy` validation:\n   ```bash\n   npx hardhat run scripts/validate-upgrade.ts --network mainnet-fork\n   ```\n2. **Storage Gap Usage:** Ensure the new implementation uses the reserved gap (line 96 shows `uint256[49] private __gap;`):\n   - Add new royalty storage to the gap\n   - Example: `uint256[47] private __gap;` if adding 2 new uint256 variables\n3. **Manual Slot Verification:** Before deployment, compare slot assignments using Hardhat storage layout:\n   ```bash\n   npx hardhat storage:layout contracts/core/ShowPass.sol > v1-layout.json\n   npx hardhat storage:layout contracts/core/ShowPassV2.sol > v2-layout.json\n   diff v1-layout.json v2-layout.json\n   ```\n\n### 4.2 Risk: ABI Mismatch During Transition Window\n\n**Severity:** HIGH\n**Likelihood:** High (if deployment sequence is not followed precisely)\n\n**Description:**\nDuring the window between on-chain upgrade completion and backend deployment, the backend will be calling the new implementation with the old ABI. This causes:\n- Transactions reverting if function selectors changed\n- Incorrect data returned if return types changed\n- Events being missed or misinterpreted\n\n**Affected Code (`contract.mapper.ts`, lines 137-141):**\n```typescript\nconst data = contract.interface.encodeFunctionData('safeMint', [\n  params.to,\n  params.tokenId,\n  params.metadataURI,\n]);\n```\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Blue-Green Deployment:** Prepare a fully tested backend container image with the new ABI BEFORE executing the on-chain upgrade. Execute deployments in this order:\n   - Deploy new backend (stays dormant, old backend handles traffic)\n   - Execute on-chain upgrade\n   - Immediately switch traffic to new backend (< 30 second window)\n2. **Feature Flag:** Add a runtime feature flag that temporarily disables all `ShowPass` write operations during the upgrade window:\n   ```typescript\n   if (config.UPGRADE_IN_PROGRESS) {\n     throw new ServiceUnavailableError('Upgrade in progress');\n   }\n   ```\n3. **Contract Version Check:** Add a `version()` function to the implementation and verify it in the backend before critical operations:\n   ```typescript\n   const version = await showPassContract.version();\n   if (version !== EXPECTED_VERSION) {\n     throw new ContractVersionMismatchError();\n   }\n   ```\n\n### 4.3 Risk: Event Listener Desynchronization\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Likelihood:** High\n\n**Description:**\nThe `stage-event.observer.ts` subscribes to on-chain events. If the new implementation emits different events (new events, changed event signatures), the observer will:\n- Miss new events entirely (falls to `default` case, line 78-82)\n- Fail to parse changed events (TypeError in argument destructuring)\n\n**Affected Code (`stage-event.observer.ts`, lines 74-76):**\n```typescript\ncase PassStakedEvent:\n  await this.handlePassStaked(event as PassStakedEvent);\n  break;\n```\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Event Schema Versioning:** Maintain backward compatibility by:\n   - Never removing existing events\n   - Only adding new optional indexed parameters\n   - If signature must change, emit both old and new events during transition\n2. **Graceful Degradation:** Update the `default` case to log unknown events rather than silently ignore:\n   ```typescript\n   default:\n     this.logger.warn(\n       `[StageEventObserver] Unhandled event: ${event.name}`,\n       { eventType: event.constructor.name, payload: event }\n     );\n   ```\n3. **Event Replay Capability:** After upgrade, replay events from the upgrade block to catch any missed during the transition:\n   ```typescript\n   await replayEventsFromBlock(upgradeBlockNumber, showPassContract);\n   ```\n\n### 4.4 Risk: Service Downtime During Upgrade\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Likelihood:** Medium\n\n**Description:**\nThe 48-hour timelock creates a known upgrade window. Without proper coordination, users may experience failed transactions or inconsistent UI states.\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Maintenance Mode:** Implement a platform-wide maintenance mode that:\n   - Shows a banner on the frontend 24 hours before upgrade\n   - Disables ShowPass-related mutations during upgrade window\n   - Displays a progress indicator during the 48-hour timelock\n2. **Read-Only Operations:** Ensure all read operations (balance queries, metadata fetches) continue working during upgrade\n3. **Transaction Queuing:** Queue pending transactions and replay them after upgrade completes\n\n### 4.5 Risk: Rollback Complexity\n\n**Severity:** HIGH\n**Likelihood:** Low (but catastrophic if needed)\n\n**Description:**\nIf the upgraded implementation contains a critical bug, rolling back requires:\n- Emergency multisig action (5/9 according to `UPGRADEABILITY_GUIDE.md`)\n- Potentially incompatible storage if the new contract wrote data in a new format\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Emergency Rollback Script:** Prepare and test a rollback script BEFORE deployment:\n   ```typescript\n   // scripts/rollback.ts (from UPGRADEABILITY_GUIDE.md, lines 216-233)\n   const proxy = TransparentUpgradeableProxy__factory.connect(proxyAddr, admin);\n   await proxy.upgradeTo(prevImpl);\n   ```\n2. **Storage Snapshot:** Take a storage snapshot of the proxy before upgrade using `eth_getStorageAt` for critical slots\n3. **Staged Rollout:** Deploy to testnet first, wait 7 days, then deploy to mainnet\n4. **Bug Bounty:** Announce the upgrade publicly and offer a bug bounty during the timelock period\n\n---\n\n## 5.
